def idx(i: int):
	return i - 1

a21,a11,a22,a33,a44,b31,b11,b22,b33,c41,c11,c22,c12,c55,c51,d11,d1,d2,d3,d4,d5=var("a21,a11,a22,a33,a44,b31,b11,b22,b33,c41,c11,c22,c12,c55,c51,d11,d1,d2,d3,d4,d5")

list_vars: list = [a21,a11,a22,a33,a44,b31,b11,b22,b33,c41,c11,c22,c12,c55,c51,d11,d1,d2,d3,d4,d5]

def is_constraint(exp):
	vars = exp.variables()

	for v in list_vars:
		if v in vars:
			return True

lambda1,lambda2,lambda3,lambda4,lambda5=var("lambda1,lambda2,lambda3,lambda4,lambda5")

N_2_0=matrix(SR, 15, 15, 1)
N_2_0[idx(2),idx(6)]=a21
N_2_0[idx(7),idx(10)]=a21
